If you have specific length concerns, message us on WhatsApp and we will guide you.</p><p class="text-[10px] text-ink-faint uppercase tracking-wider mt-3 font-medium">Fit &amp; Sizing</p></div></div></div><div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-surface-subtle overflow-hidden shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-shadow" itemScope="" itemType="https://schema.org/Question"><button class="w-full flex items-center justify-between px-6 py-5 text-left gap-4" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="faq-answer-blouse-stitching"><span class="font-serif text-base font-semibold text-ink" itemProp="name">Does the saree come with a stitched blouse?</span><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-chevron-down text-ink-faint flex-shrink-0 transition-transform duration-300" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"></path></svg></button></div><div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-surface-subtle overflow-hidden shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-shadow" itemScope="" itemType="https://schema.org/Question"><button class="w-full flex items-center justify-between px-6 py-5 text-left gap-4" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="faq-answer-first-time-drape"><span class="font-serif text-base font-semibold text-ink" itemProp="name">I have never draped a saree before.
